--- Break Even Point ---

In this lecture we will understand about break even point b e p c when a manufacturer does production
or sells his item, he is interested in knowing that what should be the value or what should be the
number of items which he should produce or he should sell so that he gets the profit and that value,
that number of items is called break even point or break even value.
We know that calculus is very much helpful in finding out the rate of change.
Suppose in mechanics we want to know the acceleration.
Then rate of change of velocity we know is acceleration.
So by differentiating velocity with respect to time we can find the acceleration.
And same is the case in commerce economics or say in business.
Here also if we differentiate the total cost with respect to the number of items which are produced
or marketed, then we can always find the marginal value.
Marginal cost.
So here calculus is very important.
We will start our discussion with first the terms, the important terms, the definition of which and
the meaning of which we should know, so that we can move further in our studies in data science.
So let us understand those terms first.
First is the cost function.
The cost function is represented by c x and it is composed of sum of two terms f plus v x f is representing
the fixed cost, like the cost of rent of the premises or the land, and v x is the variable cost.
So f is the fixed cost, v x is the variable cost, x is the number of items which are produced or which
are marketed which are sold.
Second is the demand function.
Demand function is represented by x is equal to f.
P means it is a function of price, price per unit, more the price and lesser will be the demand.
So here x is the number of units demanded by the consumer the customer and p is the price per unit.
Agreed.
Third we should know is the revenue function.
Revenue function is represented by r x which is product of p into x, p is the price per unit and x
is the number of units sold.
Or say marketed.
Then comes the profit function.
Profit is revenue function minus cost function.
So p x profit function is revenue function minus cost.
Cost function or break even point.
Profit function p x is equal to zero, so that this function r x minus c x is equal to zero means r
x is equal to c x.
At this level, when revenue and cost function both are same, we know that the profit is zero.
So on this basis we will be solving few questions so that the concept is further clear to us that.
How to find the break even point.

--- Concepts of Maxima and Minima ---

In this lecture, we are going to discuss a very important topic of calculus that is maxima and minima.
This particular concept of calculus is used in many spheres, be it commerce and industry management,
business biology or C subjects like economics, engineering and many other.
So it is a very important topic of data science and data analysts to know about this particular topic
here.
We are not going to discuss the four part of mathematics which is giving the condition of this particular
